EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

证据规模

38 条 finding / 38 处引用

主要边界

当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

适用建设

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

值得借鉴

  • 终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。
  • CodeWhale 不是把安全、长任务和扩展散落在命令行参数里，而是先形成一份“本次会话的有效配置”，后面的 turn、工具和子 Agent 都从这份配置派生。
  • 它先把“这次请求到底用哪个模型、哪些工具、什么权限”钉住，再让模型开跑；失败不会把会话炸掉，也不会误判成完成。

需要警惕

  • 当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证
  • 当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证
  • 当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

01 · TECHNICAL MAPS

三张可交互技术图，而不是一个五节点占位图

图中的节点来自 finding 的源码路径；点击节点可查看事实、固定提交和行号，搜索、缩放、主题切换均在图内完成。

02 · COVERAGE MAP

覆盖维度与证据等级

架构与所有权verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

事件驱动 Core、EngineConfig、宿主 turn admission、工具目录单一装配点与异常收尾。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

turn 流式循环、steer、流重试、工具调用预算、reasoning/prefix 稳定检查与请求快照。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

预算数学、working set pin、工具调用配对、摘要 ladder、live state rehydrate 与机械 fallback。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

immutable prefix、tool catalog LRU、goal continuation 与压缩后继任者 brief。

工具注册与执行verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

Typed ToolSpec/Registry、prepare、approval、并行资源、heartbeat、MCP/插件执行与审计。

执行环境与沙箱verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

workspace-write 策略、Seatbelt、可选 bubblewrap、外部 OpenSandbox 与 fail-open 条件。

安全、审批与权限verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

ExecPolicy 分层规则、ExecutionEnvelope 能力分类、子进程 authority envelope 与路径边界。

连接器、插件与 MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

stdio/Streamable HTTP/SSE/OAuth、secret placeholder、body cap、reviewed plugin recheck 与连接池。

全部指令与扩展面verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

instructions 配置、兼容/owned Skills roots、SKILL.md parser、插件 trust/enable 与 Hook gates。

协作与子 AgentverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

agent/Fleet roles、mailbox、预算/深度/超时、resident lease、Git worktree 与本地/SSH worker host。

观测、日志与回放verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

Session 原子保存/恢复、SQLite WAL/JSONL projection、side-git snapshots、typed events、cost receipts 与 tool audit。

恢复、测试与取舍verifiedL1 / L3

compaction、sandbox、execution envelope、plugin、Skills、MCP、subagent 等模块带有大量契约测试；本报告不把测试通过推成生产安全保证。

视频与字幕

把这次源码阅读做成可复习的短视频

视频只承诺页面和源码中能证明的事实；脚本、画面、SRT 与成片会在同一个视频目录中按版本留痕。

03 · COURSE ROUTE

十章教程不是摘要，而是源码阅读路线

每章会从本项目的多个 finding 中挑出证据，先讲问题，再给比喻，然后把实现、边界、图谱和练习串起来。

M01

总览：先知道它解决什么问题

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

M02

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/core/mod.rs:1–15 · 4 条证据
M03

主循环：任务为什么会继续推进

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/core/mod.rs:1–15 · 3 条证据
M04

模型与消息：流式结果如何进入状态

模型还在思考时，用户可以插话；子 Agent 做完的结果会在下一次请求前被父 Agent 看见；工具调用总数和流断线重试都有单轮账本。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/core/engine/turn_loop.rs:364–412 · 7 条证据
M05

工具：Agent 的手脚怎样被注册

工具不是只有一个名字和一个函数；系统会问“这一次具体参数是否只读、能否并行、需要什么审批、会占什么资源”。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/tools/spec.rs:1158–1217 · 5 条证据
M06

上下文：长任务怎样不丢重点

模型还在思考时，用户可以插话；子 Agent 做完的结果会在下一次请求前被父 Agent 看见；工具调用总数和流断线重试都有单轮账本。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/core/engine/turn_loop.rs:364–412 · 7 条证据
M07

安全：审批、权限和边界

默认不是“只能看到项目”，而是“能看全盘但只能写项目和指定目录”；这对分析工具方便，对密钥读取风险更敏感。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/sandbox/policy.rs:17–87 · 5 条证据
M08

扩展：MCP、Skill、插件如何接入

MCP 在这里不是一个 HTTP helper，而是有连接生命周期、能力发现、超时和认证的子系统；不同 server 的连接可以复用。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/mcp.rs:1–37 · 6 条证据
M09

协作：子 Agent 与工作流

子 Agent 不是一套旁路脚本：父子共享结构化协调协议，但子 Agent 默认不会继承主 Agent 的全权模式。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/tools/subagent/mod.rs:1–11 · 5 条证据
M10

证据与取舍：把源码结论带回自研

进程崩溃时不会留下半个 JSON；下一次加载也不会把孤儿 tool result 原样塞回 provider。

进入本章 →crates/tui/src/session_manager.rs:26–40 · 6 条证据
01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · Core 把 UI 与 AI 交互拆成事件驱动的控制面

源码事实

core/mod.rs 明确把 engine、events、ops、session、tool_parser、turn 分成独立模块，并在 TUI alt-screen 中禁止直接 print_stdout/print_stderr，要求通过 tracing 和事件通道输出。

白话解释

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自研时应先定义 operation/event 契约，再让 TUI、HTTP 和自动化入口共享同一运行时；禁止 UI 层私自执行工具或改会话。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–7
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 8–15
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · b63e48331b7d · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/tui/src/core/mod.rs:1–15固定提交 ↗
    1  //! Core engine module for `DeepSeek` CLI.
    2  //!
    3  //! This module provides the event-driven architecture that separates
    4  //! the UI from the AI interaction logic:
    5  //!
    6  //! - `engine`: The main engine that processes operations
    7  //! - `events`: Events emitted by the engine to the UI
    8  //! - `ops`: Operations submitted by the UI to the engine
    9  //! - `session`: Session state management
   10  //! - `turn`: Turn context and tracking
   11
   12  // Engine code runs inside the TUI alt-screen — see `runtime_log` for why
   13  // raw stdio prints must not appear here. Use `tracing::*` instead.
   14  #![deny(clippy::print_stdout)]
   15  #![deny(clippy::print_stderr)]
   16
   17  pub mod authority;
   18  pub mod engine;
   19  pub mod events;
   20  // The first production consumer of the staged runtime contract is the
   21  // provider-neutral model boundary. Keep the remaining contract files staged
   22  // until their own consumers land instead of compiling dead scaffolding.
   23  #[path = "runtime_contract/model.rs"]
   24  pub mod model_client;
   25  pub mod ops;
   26  #[path = "runtime_contract/termination.rs"]
   27  pub mod termination;
   28  // The rest of `runtime_contract/` stays on disk as staged Core-runtime
   29  // scaffolding and remains deliberately uncompiled until it has production
   30  // consumers (TUI-DOG-017).
   31  pub mod session;
   32  pub mod tool_parser;
   33  pub mod turn;
   34
   35  // Re-exports
   36

crates/tui/src/core/mod.rs · cfc2f2b13c07当前源码 ↗
    1  //! Core engine module for `DeepSeek` CLI.
    2  //!
    3  //! This module provides the event-driven architecture that separates
    4  //! the UI from the AI interaction logic:
    5  //!
    6  //! - `engine`: The main engine that processes operations
    7  //! - `events`: Events emitted by the engine to the UI
    8  //! - `ops`: Operations submitted by the UI to the engine
    9  //! - `session`: Session state management
   10  //! - `turn`: Turn context and tracking
   11
   12  // Engine code runs inside the TUI alt-screen — see `runtime_log` for why
   13  // raw stdio prints must not appear here. Use `tracing::*` instead.
   14  #![deny(clippy::print_stdout)]
   15  #![deny(clippy::print_stderr)]
   16
   17  pub mod authority;
   18  // `crates/core` now owns the engine (issue #5261). The TUI's `engine`
   19  // consumes the core boundary directly so headless and TUI share one
   20  // `Op`-in / `EventMsg`-out API, one `ThreadId`/`SessionId` type, and one
   21  // `Journal` shape. New code should import from `codewhale_core`.
   22  pub mod engine;
   23  pub(crate) use engine::tool_catalog::allowlist_is_native_file_and_shell_only;
   24  pub mod events;
   25  // The first production consumer of the staged runtime contract is the
   26  // provider-neutral model boundary. Keep the remaining contract files staged
   27  // until their own consumers land instead of compiling dead scaffolding.
   28  #[path = "runtime_contract/model.rs"]
   29  pub mod model_client;
   30  pub mod ops;
   31  #[path = "runtime_contract/termination.rs"]
   32  pub mod termination;
   33  // The rest of `runtime_contract/` stays on disk as staged Core-runtime
   34  // scaffolding and remains deliberately uncompiled until it has production
   35  // consumers (TUI-DOG-017).
   36  pub mod session;
   37  pub mod tool_parser;
   38  pub mod turn;
   39
   40  // Re-exports
   41
02
L1factarchitecture

02 · EngineConfig 是把能力、预算和权限拧在一起的运行时总闸

源码事实

EngineConfig 同时保存 workspace、allow_shell、trust_mode、MCP/Skills/instructions、max_steps、subagent concurrency/depth/token budget、compaction、snapshots、memory、allowed/disallowed tools、max_tool_calls、hooks、strict tool mode、network policy 与 sandbox 偏好。

白话解释

CodeWhale 不是把安全、长任务和扩展散落在命令行参数里，而是先形成一份“本次会话的有效配置”，后面的 turn、工具和子 Agent 都从这份配置派生。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自研要区分静态配置、turn authority 和工具调用 authority；不要让每个工具重新解释一遍全局配置。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 221–258
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 259–298
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  221  /// Configuration for the engine
  222  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  223  pub struct EngineConfig {
  224      /// Model identifier to use for responses.
  225      pub model: String,
  226      /// Route/offering limits for the active provider+model, when the runtime
  227      /// route resolver had concrete catalog facts.
  228      pub active_route_limits: Option<codewhale_config::route::RouteLimits>,
  229      /// Workspace root for tool execution and file operations.
  230      pub workspace: PathBuf,
  231      /// Allow shell tool execution when true.
  232      pub allow_shell: bool,
  233      /// Enable trust mode (skip approvals) when true.
  234      pub trust_mode: bool,
  235      /// Path to the notes file used by the notes tool.
  236      pub notes_path: PathBuf,
  237      /// Path to the MCP configuration file.
  238      pub mcp_config_path: PathBuf,
  239      /// Directory containing discoverable skills.
  240      pub skills_dir: PathBuf,
  241      /// Restrict skill discovery to CodeWhale-owned roots plus explicit
  242      /// `skills_dir` configuration.
  243      pub skills_scan_codewhale_only: bool,
  244      /// Immutable plugin authority snapshot scoped to `workspace`. Normal App
  245      /// hosts provide this explicitly; headless/embed callers that leave it
  246      /// unset receive a fresh workspace-specific snapshot in [`Engine::new`].
  247      pub plugin_registry: Option<Arc<crate::plugins::PluginRegistry>>,
  248      /// Sources injected as `<instructions source="…">` blocks in the system
  249      /// prompt (#454). Each entry is either a disk path (read at render time)
  250      /// or an inline string. Loaded in declared order from the user's
  251      /// `instructions = [...]` config or constructed by embedders.
  252      ///
  253      /// Generalized from `Vec<PathBuf>` so embedders can inject inline content
  254      /// without staging a disk file. `From<PathBuf>` impl keeps existing callers
  255      /// working with `.into()` at the call site.
  256      pub instructions: Vec<crate::prompts::InstructionSource>,
  257      pub project_context_pack_enabled: bool,
  258      /// When true, the model is instructed to respond in the current locale
  259      /// and a post-hoc translation layer replaces remaining English output.
  260      pub translation_enabled: bool,
  261      pub verbosity: Option<String>,
  262      /// Maximum number of assistant steps before stopping.
  263      pub max_steps: u32,
  264      /// Maximum number of concurrently active subagents.
  265      pub max_subagents: usize,
  266      /// Maximum queued + running sub-agents admitted for this engine session.
  267      pub max_admitted_subagents: usize,
  268      /// Number of direct (depth-1) sub-agents that may execute concurrently
  269      /// before further launches queue for a launch slot (#3095).
  270      /// Resolved from `[subagents] launch_concurrency`.
  271      pub launch_concurrency: usize,
  272      /// Whether the model-facing `agent` tool is available after applying
  273      /// feature flags and `[subagents]` opt-out controls.
  274      pub subagents_enabled: bool,
  275      /// Feature flags controlling tool availability.
  276      pub features: Features,
  277      /// Deterministic auto-review policy for tool calls.
  278      pub auto_review_policy: crate::tui::auto_review::AutoReviewPolicy,
  279      /// Auto-compaction settings for long conversations.
  280      pub compaction: CompactionConfig,
  281      /// Shared Todo list state.
  282      pub todos: SharedTodoList,
  283      /// Shared Plan state.
  284      pub plan_state: SharedPlanState,
  285      /// Shared runtime goal state for model-visible goal tools.
  286      pub goal_state: SharedGoalState,
  287      /// Maximum sub-agent recursion depth (default 3). See
  288      /// `SubAgentRuntime::max_spawn_depth`. Override via
  289      /// `[subagents] max_depth = N` in `~/.codewhale/config.toml`.
  290      pub max_spawn_depth: u32,
  291      /// Optional aggregate token budget for each root sub-agent run.
  292      /// Descendant agents inherit the root pool unless a child starts a new
  293      /// budget scope with an explicit per-call override.
  294      pub subagent_token_budget: Option<u64>,
  295      /// Per-domain network policy decider (#135). Shared across the session so
  296      /// session-scoped approvals (`/network allow <host>`) persist for the
  297      /// remainder of the run.
  298      pub network_policy: Option<crate::network_policy::NetworkPolicyDecider>,
  299      /// Whether to take side-git workspace snapshots before/after each turn.
  300      pub snapshots_enabled: bool,
  301      /// Maximum workspace size (in bytes) before snapshots self-disable on
  302      /// first init. `0` disables the cap. Resolved from
  303      /// `[snapshots] max_workspace_gb` × 1 GB at engine construction.
  304      pub snapshots_max_workspace_bytes: u64,
  305      /// Post-edit LSP diagnostics injection (#136). When `None`, the engine
  306      /// constructs a disabled manager so the field is always present.
  307      pub lsp_config: Option<crate::lsp::LspConfig>,
  308      /// Durable runtime services exposed to model-visible tools.
  309      pub runtime_services: RuntimeToolServices,
  310      /// Per-role/type sub-agent model overrides already resolved from config.
  311      pub subagent_model_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,
  312      /// Merged fleet roster (built-ins + config + personal/workspace agent
  313      /// files) shared by model-spawned sub-agents and fleet dispatch
  314      /// (#fleet-roster cutover (v0.8.67)). Defaults to built-ins only; the
  315      /// engine-config construction sites load it at session start and the setup
  316      /// wizard refreshes it after each successful profile save.
  317      pub fleet_roster: std::sync::Arc<crate::fleet::roster::FleetRoster>,
  318      /// Whether the user-memory feature is enabled (#489). When `true` the
  319      /// engine reads `memory_path` on each prompt assembly and prepends a
  320      /// `<user_memory>` block to the system prompt.
  321      pub memory_enabled: bool,
  322      /// When `true`, the legacy `memory.rs` push/inject path is deprecated
  323      /// in favour of Moraine MCP recall. `compose_block` returns `None`
  324      /// regardless of `memory_enabled`, the `remember` tool is not
  325      /// registered, and `# foo` quick-add falls through.
  326      pub moraine_fallback: bool,
  327      /// Path to the user memory file (#489). Always populated; only
  328      /// consulted when `memory_enabled` is `true`.
  329      pub memory_path: PathBuf,
  330      /// Default directory for Xiaomi MiMo speech/TTS tool outputs.
  331      pub speech_output_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
  332      pub vision_config: Option<crate::config::VisionModelConfig>,
  333      pub goal_objective: Option<String>,
  334      pub goal_token_budget: Option<u32>,
  335      pub goal_status: GoalStatus,
  336      /// Tool restriction from custom slash command frontmatter.
  337      /// `None` means the current turn may use the normal tool set.
  338      pub allowed_tools: Option<Vec<String>>,
  339      /// Tool deny-list.  Deny always wins over allow (#3027).
  340      /// `None` means no tools are explicitly denied.
  341      pub disallowed_tools: Option<Vec<String>>,
  342      /// Hard per-turn cap on admitted tool calls (#4415). `None` (the default)
  343      /// means unlimited and leaves the turn admission gate inert. Task hosts
  344      /// set this from the task's structured `max_tool_calls` constraint; the
  345      /// per-turn counter itself lives in the turn loop, not here.
  346      pub max_tool_calls: Option<u32>,
  347      /// Hook executor for control-plane hooks.
  348      /// `ToolCallBefore` hooks may deny a tool call with exit code 2.
  349      pub hook_executor: Option<std::sync::Arc<crate::hooks::HookExecutor>>,
  350      /// Resolved BCP-47 locale tag (e.g. `"en"`, `"zh-Hans"`, `"ja"`)
  351      /// for the `## Environment` block in the system prompt. The
  352      /// caller resolves this from `Settings` once at engine
  353      /// construction; the engine never touches disk for it.
  354      pub locale_tag: String,
  355      /// When true, force `tool_choice: "required"` and opt compatible function
  356      /// schemas into DeepSeek beta strict mode.
  357      pub strict_tool_mode: bool,
  358      /// Workshop / large-tool-output routing (#548). `None` disables routing.
  359      pub workshop: Option<crate::tools::large_output_router::WorkshopConfig>,
  360      /// Which search backend `web_search` should use. Default: DuckDuckGo.
  361      pub search_provider: crate::config::SearchProvider,
  362      /// API key for Tavily, Bocha, Metaso, Baidu, Volcengine, or Sofya.
  363      /// `None` for Bing, DuckDuckGo, or SearXNG.
  364      /// Metaso also falls back to the `METASO_API_KEY` env var.
  365      /// Baidu also falls back to `BAIDU_SEARCH_API_KEY`.
  366      pub search_api_key: Option<String>,
  367      /// Optional DuckDuckGo-compatible HTML endpoint override.
  368      pub search_base_url: Option<String>,
  369      /// Per-step DeepSeek API timeout for sub-agent `create_message` requests.

  221  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  222  pub struct EngineConfig {
  223      /// Model identifier to use for responses.
  224      pub model: String,
  225      /// Route/offering limits for the active provider+model, when the runtime
  226      /// route resolver had concrete catalog facts.
  227      pub active_route_limits: Option<codewhale_config::route::RouteLimits>,
  228      /// Workspace root for tool execution and file operations.
  229      pub workspace: PathBuf,
  230      /// Optional host-owned root for delegated-agent runtime state.
  231      ///
  232      /// When unset, the worker ledger, complete transcript artifacts and
  233      /// coordination lock retain their historical location under
  234      /// `workspace/.codewhale/state`. Embedders may set a session-scoped root
  235      /// to separate that control-plane state from the execution workspace.
  236      /// Child cwd and file authority still derive from `workspace`; hosts using
  237      /// distinct state roots for the same workspace must coordinate conflicting
  238      /// writes themselves or isolate writers with worktrees.
  239      pub subagent_state_root: Option<PathBuf>,
  240      /// Allow shell tool execution when true.
  241      pub allow_shell: bool,
  242      /// Enable trust mode (skip approvals) when true.
  243      pub trust_mode: bool,
  244      /// Path to the notes file used by the notes tool.
  245      pub notes_path: PathBuf,
  246      /// Path to the MCP configuration file.
  247      pub mcp_config_path: PathBuf,
  248      /// Directory containing discoverable skills.
  249      pub skills_dir: PathBuf,
  250      /// Restrict skill discovery to CodeWhale-owned roots plus explicit
  251      /// `skills_dir` configuration.
  252      pub skills_scan_codewhale_only: bool,
  253      /// Immutable plugin authority snapshot scoped to `workspace`. Normal App
  254      /// hosts provide this explicitly; headless/embed callers that leave it
  255      /// unset receive a fresh workspace-specific snapshot in [`Engine::new`].
  256      pub plugin_registry: Option<Arc<crate::plugins::PluginRegistry>>,

  258      /// prompt (#454). Each entry is either a disk path (read at render time)
  259      /// or an inline string. Loaded in declared order from the user's
  260      /// `instructions = [...]` config or constructed by embedders.

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · Turn 运行前冻结事实，运行后再做持久化与继续决策

源码事实

Engine::run 在每个 operation 前应用待处理 runtime authority；SendMessage 路径先解析路由、输入策略、tool surface 和快照，再进入 turn loop；异常会被 catch_unwind 转成失败 TurnComplete，成功完成才触发 goal continuation，post-turn snapshot 在完成事件后异步执行。

白话解释

它先把“这次请求到底用哪个模型、哪些工具、什么权限”钉住，再让模型开跑；失败不会把会话炸掉，也不会误判成完成。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长任务系统需要 pre-dispatch receipt、失败终态和 post-turn checkpoint 三个明确边界，不能只靠一个 finally。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1920–1930
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1931–1942
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

L1factarchitecture

04 · Tool catalog 与 preview 共享同一装配函数，但 preview 是纯观察

源码事实

build_turn_tool_registry_and_catalog 被标为下一请求工具注册表和目录的唯一权威；live 模式允许连接 MCP、创建 mailbox、fork context 和 subagent runtime，preview 模式使用 Inert/PassiveSnapshot，不连接、不启动、不写 engine state，却输出同一 catalog 投影。

白话解释

用户点“查看这次会发给模型什么工具”时，不会因为预览动作偷偷启动 MCP 或子 Agent；预览和真实请求走同一个拼装逻辑，减少两套实现漂移。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Harness 应提供 side-effect-free request manifest，既服务调试也能做审批前可解释性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 3385–3400
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 3401–3418
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 3385      /// Build the turn's tool registry and the model-facing tool catalog.
 3386      ///
 3387      /// This is the single authority for "what tools would the next request
 3388      /// carry". `handle_send_message` calls it with [`SubAgentWiring::Live`]
 3389      /// and [`McpAccess::Connect`]; `/preview-request` calls it with
 3390      /// [`SubAgentWiring::Inert`] and [`McpAccess::PassiveSnapshot`], which
 3391      /// together remove every side effect of the build — no fork snapshot, no
 3392      /// spawned mailbox drainer, no pool creation, no `connect_all`, no status
 3393      /// events — while producing a byte-identical catalog for the state that
 3394      /// is already live.
 3395      ///
 3396      /// The session's `last_tool_catalog` is never an acceptable substitute:
 3397      /// it is one turn stale and stores the pre-activation catalog rather than
 3398      /// the active subset the provider would actually receive.
 3399      ///
 3400      /// `allowed_tools` is the command-scoped allow-list gate the catalog is
 3401      /// filtered under. It is an explicit **parameter**, not a read of
 3402      /// `self.config.allowed_tools`, because the preview's gate belongs to a
 3403      /// turn that has not been installed: writing it onto the engine and
 3404      /// restoring it afterwards would leave the wrong gate installed across
 3405      /// every `.await` in this function, and would leave it installed
 3406      /// permanently if the task were cancelled or panicked between the two
 3407      /// writes.

 3388      /// Build the turn's tool registry and the model-facing tool catalog.
 3389      ///
 3390      /// This is the single authority for "what tools would the next request
 3391      /// carry". `handle_send_message` calls it with [`SubAgentWiring::Live`]
 3392      /// and [`McpAccess::Connect`]; `/preview-request` calls it with
 3393      /// [`SubAgentWiring::Inert`] and [`McpAccess::PassiveSnapshot`], which
 3394      /// together remove every side effect of the build — no fork snapshot, no
 3395      /// spawned mailbox drainer, no pool creation, no `connect_all`, no status
 3396      /// events — while producing a byte-identical catalog for the state that
 3397      /// is already live.
 3398      ///
 3399      /// The session's `last_tool_catalog` is never an acceptable substitute:
 3400      /// it is one turn stale and stores the pre-activation catalog rather than
 3401      /// the active subset the provider would actually receive.
 3402      ///
 3403      /// `allowed_tools` is the command-scoped allow-list gate the catalog is
 3404      /// filtered under. It is an explicit **parameter**, not a read of
 3405      /// `self.config.allowed_tools`, because the preview's gate belongs to a
 3406      /// turn that has not been installed: writing it onto the engine and
 3407      /// restoring it afterwards would leave the wrong gate installed across
 3408      /// every `.await` in this function, and would leave it installed
 3409      /// permanently if the task were cancelled or panicked between the two
 3410      /// writes.

03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

7 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · 单轮流式循环有取消、steer、工具预算和子 Agent 结果注入

源码事实

handle_deepseek_turn 为一个外部用户 turn 保留 StuckGuard、ReadRepeatGuard、context recovery counter、ToolCallBudget、goal continuation counter 和 stream retry counter；每次 provider 请求前检查取消、runtime authority、steer 输入、子 Agent completion、max steps 与目标 token budget。

白话解释

模型还在思考时，用户可以插话；子 Agent 做完的结果会在下一次请求前被父 Agent 看见；工具调用总数和流断线重试都有单轮账本。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Steer、cancel 和 child completion 都应在 provider request boundary 注入，才能避免“已取消但又发了一次请求”。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 364–387
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 388–412
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · prefix cache 不是口号，而是每次请求前的可诊断一致性检查

源码事实

turn loop 在构造 request 前用 PrefixStabilityManager 对 system prompt 和 active tools 做 fingerprint check，发出 changed/stable 事件；随后还用 frozen_prefix 做三段式 immutable prefix 校验，记录 drift 而不默默重写历史。

白话解释

工具排序、描述或系统提示一变，CodeWhale 会知道 DeepSeek 的 KV 前缀可能失效，而不是把缓存 miss 当成模型随机变慢。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自研时应把缓存稳定性做成可观察指标，并把 system/tool catalog 与 append-only history 分区。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 620–652
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 653–687
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  664                                  pinned_combined_hash: pinned_hash,
  665                              })
  666                              .await;

  677                                  description: String::new(),
  678                                  system_prompt_changed: false,
  679                                  tools_changed: false,
  680                                  stability_pct: (pm.stability_ratio() * 100.0).round() as u32,

  683                              })
  684                              .await;
  685                      }

  700                  Some(frozen) => {
  701                      if let Err(drift) = frozen.verify(&system_text, current_tools) {

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · ContextBudget 用饱和数学先给输出留空间，再决定压缩

源码事实

ContextBudget 计算 route window、已用输入、clamped output cap、headroom、可用 input、75% compaction trigger 和 Low/Medium/High/Critical pressure；所有减法使用 saturating arithmetic，避免小窗口被大输出预留打成负预算。

白话解释

模型要回答的空间先保留，输入预算才是剩下的；即使配置了一个夸张的输出上限，也不会把可用输入预算算成负数。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文工程的第一层不是摘要，而是一个独立、可单测、不会下溢的预算模块。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–15
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 16–32
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · b63e48331b7d · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/tui/src/context_budget.rs:1–32固定提交 ↗
    1  //! Unified context-budget math for the TUI.
    2  //!
    3  //! Given a model's context window, the current input token estimate, and a
    4  //! configured output cap, [`ContextBudget`] derives the four numbers the rest
    5  //! of the app needs to reason about a turn:
    6  //!
    7  //!   * **available input budget** — how many input tokens may still be spent
    8  //!     after reserving room for the model's output;
    9  //!   * **output token cap** — the output reservation actually used to compute
   10  //!     that budget (clamped so it never starves the window);
   11  //!   * **compaction trigger** — the input-token level at which compaction
   12  //!     should be suggested (default: ~75% of the spendable input ceiling);
   13  //!   * **[`PressureLevel`]** — a coarse Low/Medium/High/Critical signal the UI
   14  //!     can render without re-deriving thresholds.
   15  //!
   16  //! This module is the budget-math *foundation*. It is intentionally pure (no
   17  //! I/O, no clock, no engine/config types) so it can be unit-tested in isolation
   18  //! and later consumed by the engine capacity checkpoints and the TUI pressure
   19  //! indicator. Those consumers are wired in a separate pass; nothing here calls
   20  //! into them.
   21  //!
   22  //! ### Why the output reservation is window-dependent
   23  //!
   24  //! The engine's existing input-budget helper
   25  //! (`core::engine::context::context_input_budget_for_window`) computes
   26  //! `window - reserved_output - headroom` and learned the hard way that
   27  //! reserving a large fixed output (262K for V4-class interleaved thinking) on a
   28  //! *small* self-hosted window (e.g. a 256K vLLM deployment) underflows to a
   29  //! negative budget and silently disables every preflight/recovery path. We
   30  //! mirror that lesson here with saturating arithmetic and an output cap that is
   31  //! always clamped to leave at least [`MIN_INPUT_BUDGET_TOKENS`] of input room,
   32  //! so the budget can never collapse to zero on a legitimately sized window.
   33
   34  // Foundation module: the public surface is exercised by unit tests but is not
   35  // yet referenced by the engine capacity checkpoints or the TUI pressure
   36  // indicator (those consumers are wired in a later pass). Allow dead_code so the
   37  // substrate can land warning-clean ahead of its callers, matching how other
   38  // not-yet-wired primitives in this crate are gated.
   39  //
   40  // Note: the context report now consumes `PressureLevel::from_usage_percent` and
   41  // `label`, but the rest of the substrate (`ContextBudget` and its methods,
   42  // `PressureLevel::suggests_compaction`) is still pending its engine/TUI
   43  // consumers, so the blanket allow stays until those land.
   44  #![allow(dead_code)]
   45
   46  /// Fraction of the window, expressed as a percentage, at or above which
   47  /// compaction should be suggested. Mirrors the "high" pressure boundary the
   48  /// existing context report uses for its diagnostic label, rounded up to the
   49  /// conventional three-quarters-full trigger.
   50  pub const DEFAULT_COMPACTION_TRIGGER_PERCENT: f64 = 75.0;
   51
   52  /// Percentage of the window at or above which pressure is [`PressureLevel::Critical`].
   53  pub const CRITICAL_PRESSURE_PERCENT: f64 = 90.0;
   54
   55  /// Percentage of the window at or above which pressure is [`PressureLevel::High`].
   56  /// Pressure remains a window-relative UI signal. The actual compaction trigger
   57  /// is relative to the spendable input ceiling, so these thresholds can diverge
   58  /// when output reservation consumes a substantial part of the window.
   59  pub const HIGH_PRESSURE_PERCENT: f64 = DEFAULT_COMPACTION_TRIGGER_PERCENT;
   60
   61  /// Percentage of the window at or above which pressure is [`PressureLevel::Medium`].
   62  /// Matches the "moderate" boundary of the existing diagnostic report.
   63  pub const MEDIUM_PRESSURE_PERCENT: f64 = 40.0;
   64
   65  /// Safety headroom (tokens) subtracted from the window in addition to the
   66  /// reserved output, to avoid bumping a provider's hard limit. Matches the
   67  /// engine's `CONTEXT_HEADROOM_TOKENS`.
   68  pub const CONTEXT_HEADROOM_TOKENS: u64 = 1_024;
   69
   70  /// Smallest input budget (tokens) [`ContextBudget`] will report for any window
   71  /// large enough to hold it. The output cap is clamped down as needed to
   72  /// preserve this much input room, so a generous configured output cap can never
   73  /// drive the available input budget to zero on a usable window.
   74  pub const MIN_INPUT_BUDGET_TOKENS: u64 = 1_024;
   75
   76  /// Coarse, UI-facing description of how full the context window is.
   77  ///
   78  /// Ordered from least to most pressure so the variants can be compared
   79  /// (`level >= PressureLevel::High`) and so the derived `Ord` matches intuition.
   80  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
   81  pub enum PressureLevel {
   82      /// Plenty of room; nothing to surface.
   83      Low,
   84      /// Noticeably filling up; informational.
   85      Medium,
   86      /// At or past the compaction trigger; suggest compaction.
   87      High,
   88      /// Near the window limit; compaction/clear is urgent.
   89      Critical,
   90  }
   91
   92  impl PressureLevel {
   93      /// Classify a window-usage percentage (0.0..=100.0) into a level.
   94      ///
   95      /// Inputs outside the range are clamped, so callers may pass a raw
   96      /// percentage without pre-validating it.
   97      #[must_use]
   98      pub fn from_usage_percent(percent: f64) -> Self {
   99          let percent = percent.clamp(0.0, 100.0);
  100          if percent >= CRITICAL_PRESSURE_PERCENT {
  101              PressureLevel::Critical
  102          } else if percent >= HIGH_PRESSURE_PERCENT {
  103              PressureLevel::High

crates/tui/src/context_budget.rs · cfc2f2b13c07当前源码 ↗
    1  //! Unified context-budget math for the TUI.
    2  //!
    3  //! Given a model's context window, the current input token estimate, and a
    4  //! configured output cap, [`ContextBudget`] derives the four numbers the rest
    5  //! of the app needs to reason about a turn:
    6  //!
    7  //!   * **available input budget** — how many input tokens may still be spent
    8  //!     after reserving room for the model's output;
    9  //!   * **output token cap** — the output reservation actually used to compute
   10  //!     that budget (clamped so it never starves the window);
   11  //!   * **compaction trigger** — the input-token level at which compaction
   12  //!     should be suggested (default: ~75% of the full window, clamped to the
   13  //!     spendable input ceiling);
   14  //!   * **[`PressureLevel`]** — a coarse Low/Medium/High/Critical signal the UI
   15  //!     can render without re-deriving thresholds.
   16  //!
   17  //! This module is the budget-math *foundation*. It is intentionally pure (no
   18  //! I/O, no clock, no engine/config types) so it can be unit-tested in isolation.
   19  //! Its consumers live outside it and call in, never the reverse: `route_budget`
   20  //! and `core::engine::context` for [`ContextBudget`], `context_report` for
   21  //! [`PressureLevel`].
   22  //!
   23  //! ### Why the output reservation is window-dependent
   24  //!
   25  //! The engine's existing input-budget helper
   26  //! (`core::engine::context::context_input_budget_for_window`) computes
   27  //! `window - reserved_output - headroom` and learned the hard way that
   28  //! reserving a large fixed output (262K for V4-class interleaved thinking) on a
   29  //! *small* self-hosted window (e.g. a 256K vLLM deployment) underflows to a
   30  //! negative budget and silently disables every preflight/recovery path. We
   31  //! mirror that lesson here with saturating arithmetic and an output cap that is
   32  //! always clamped to leave at least [`MIN_INPUT_BUDGET_TOKENS`] of input room,
   33  //! so the budget can never collapse to zero on a legitimately sized window.
   34
   35  // This module IS wired. `ContextBudget` is consumed by `route_budget.rs` and
   36  // `core/engine/context.rs`; `PressureLevel` by `context_report.rs`. It sits on
   37  // the do-not-delete list in AGENTS.md because a blanket `allow(dead_code)` here,
   38  // plus a comment that used to claim the module was "not yet referenced," taught
   39  // several dead-code audits to propose deleting a live file. The allow is now
   40  // per-item on the three genuinely-unused methods, so anything that goes dead
   41  // here shows up as a warning instead of hiding behind a module-wide waiver.
   42
   43  /// Fraction of the window, expressed as a percentage, at or above which
   44  /// compaction should be suggested. Mirrors the "high" pressure boundary the
   45  /// existing context report uses for its diagnostic label, rounded up to the
   46  /// conventional three-quarters-full trigger.
   47  pub const DEFAULT_COMPACTION_TRIGGER_PERCENT: f64 = 75.0;
   48
   49  /// Percentage of the window at or above which pressure is [`PressureLevel::Critical`].
   50  pub const CRITICAL_PRESSURE_PERCENT: f64 = 90.0;
   51
   52  /// Percentage of the window at or above which pressure is [`PressureLevel::High`].
   53  /// Pressure and the requested compaction trigger are window-relative. The
   54  /// trigger is clamped to the spendable input ceiling, so it can fire before
   55  /// this UI boundary when output reservation consumes substantial window space.
   56  pub const HIGH_PRESSURE_PERCENT: f64 = DEFAULT_COMPACTION_TRIGGER_PERCENT;
   57
   58  /// Percentage of the window at or above which pressure is [`PressureLevel::Medium`].
   59  /// Matches the "moderate" boundary of the existing diagnostic report.
   60  pub const MEDIUM_PRESSURE_PERCENT: f64 = 40.0;
   61
   62  /// Safety headroom (tokens) subtracted from the window in addition to the
   63  /// reserved output, to avoid bumping a provider's hard limit. Matches the
   64  /// engine's `CONTEXT_HEADROOM_TOKENS`.
   65  pub const CONTEXT_HEADROOM_TOKENS: u64 = 1_024;
   66
   67  /// Smallest input budget (tokens) [`ContextBudget`] will report for any window
   68  /// large enough to hold it. The output cap is clamped down as needed to
   69  /// preserve this much input room, so a generous configured output cap can never
   70  /// drive the available input budget to zero on a usable window.
   71  pub const MIN_INPUT_BUDGET_TOKENS: u64 = 1_024;
   72
   73  /// Coarse, UI-facing description of how full the context window is.
   74  ///
   75  /// Ordered from least to most pressure so the variants can be compared
   76  /// (`level >= PressureLevel::High`) and so the derived `Ord` matches intuition.
   77  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
   78  pub enum PressureLevel {
   79      /// Plenty of room; nothing to surface.
   80      Low,
   81      /// Noticeably filling up; informational.
   82      Medium,
   83      /// At or past the compaction trigger; suggest compaction.
   84      High,
   85      /// Near the window limit; compaction/clear is urgent.
   86      Critical,
   87  }
   88
   89  impl PressureLevel {
   90      /// Classify a window-usage percentage (0.0..=100.0) into a level.
   91      ///
   92      /// Inputs outside the range are clamped, so callers may pass a raw
   93      /// percentage without pre-validating it.
   94      #[must_use]
   95      pub fn from_usage_percent(percent: f64) -> Self {
   96          let percent = percent.clamp(0.0, 100.0);
   97          if percent >= CRITICAL_PRESSURE_PERCENT {
   98              PressureLevel::Critical
   99          } else if percent >= HIGH_PRESSURE_PERCENT {
  100              PressureLevel::High
  101          } else if percent >= MEDIUM_PRESSURE_PERCENT {
  102              PressureLevel::Medium
  103          } else {
04
L1factmodelContext

04 · 压缩先保留尾部、工作集、错误和补丁，再维护工具调用配对

源码事实

plan_compaction 会固定最近消息，依据 repo-aware working set 和外部 pin 保留路径，识别错误/patch，最后通过 fixpoint 规则确保 assistant tool call 与 tool result 不被拆开；用户消息始终保留。

白话解释

压缩不是从前往后粗暴删消息：正在改的文件、刚出现的错误、补丁和最近对话会被钉住，工具调用的“发票”和“回执”也不能只剩一半。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文压缩必须是结构化 plan + invariant，而非一个 `messages.slice()`。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 473–489
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 490–507
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  473  fn should_pin_message(text: &str, working_set_paths: &HashSet<String>) -> bool {
  474      let lower = text.to_lowercase();
  475
  476      let mentions_working_set = working_set_paths.iter().any(|p| text.contains(p));
  477      if mentions_working_set {
  478          return true;
  479      }
  480
  481      let error_markers = [
  482          "error:",
  483          "error ",
  484          "failed",
  485          "panic",
  486          "traceback",
  487          "stack trace",
  488          "assertion failed",
  489          "test failed",
  490      ];
  491      if error_markers.iter().any(|m| lower.contains(m)) {
  492          return true;
  493      }
  494
  495      let patch_markers = [
  496          "diff --git",
  497          "+++ b/",
  498          "--- a/",
  499          "*** begin patch",
  500          "*** update file:",
  501          "*** add file:",
  502          "*** delete file:",
  503          "```diff",
  504          "apply_patch",
  505      ];
  506      patch_markers.iter().any(|m| lower.contains(m))
  507  }
  508
  509  pub fn plan_compaction(
  510      messages: &[Message],
  511      workspace: Option<&Path>,
  512      keep_recent: usize,
  513      external_pins: Option<&[usize]>,
  514      external_working_set_paths: Option<&[String]>,
  515  ) -> CompactionPlan {
  516      let mut pinned_indices: BTreeSet<usize> = BTreeSet::new();
  517      let len = messages.len();
  518      if len == 0 {
  519          return CompactionPlan::default();
  520      }
  521
  522      // Always pin the tail of the conversation to preserve immediate context.
  523      let recent_start = len.saturating_sub(keep_recent);
  524      pinned_indices.extend(recent_start..len);
  525
  526      // Derive a repo-aware working set from recent messages/tool calls and
  527      // merge it with any externally provided working-set paths.
  528      let seed_indices = external_pins.unwrap_or(&[]);
  529      let mut working_set_paths = derive_working_set_paths(messages, workspace, seed_indices);
  530      if let Some(paths) = external_working_set_paths {
  531          for path in paths {
  532              if let Some(normalized) = normalize_path_candidate(path, workspace)
  533                  && is_working_set_path(&normalized)
  534              {
  535                  let _ = working_set_paths.insert(normalized);
  536              }
  537          }
  538      }
  539
  540      for (idx, msg) in messages.iter().enumerate() {
  541          if pinned_indices.contains(&idx) {
  542              continue;
  543          }
  544          let text = message_text(msg);
  545          if should_pin_message(&text, &working_set_paths) {
  546              pinned_indices.insert(idx);
  547          }
  548      }
  549
  550      // External pins are authoritative and should be preserved even if they
  551      // were not detected by the heuristics above.
  552      if let Some(pins) = external_pins {
  553          pinned_indices.extend(pins.iter().copied().filter(|idx| *idx < len));
  554      }
  555
  556      // Ensure tool result messages are not kept without their corresponding tool call.
  557      enforce_tool_call_pairs(messages, &mut pinned_indices);
  558
  559      // Some OpenAI-compatible chat templates require at least one user text
  560      // message. Tool-heavy tails can otherwise compact down to only tool calls
  561      // and tool results, which makes those backends reject the next request.
  562      if !pinned_indices
  563          .iter()
  564          .any(|&idx| is_user_text_query(&messages[idx]))
  565          && let Some(idx) = messages
  566              .iter()
  567              .enumerate()
  568              .rev()
  569              .find_map(|(idx, msg)| is_user_text_query(msg).then_some(idx))
  570      {
  571          pinned_indices.insert(idx);
  572      }
  573
  574      let summarize_indices = (0..len)
  575          .filter(|idx| !pinned_indices.contains(idx))
  576          .collect();
  577
  578      // `working_set_paths` was used only for pinning decisions above.

  473  /// pressure gate, mirroring [`compaction_pressure_reached_with_billed`].
  474  pub fn should_compact_with_billed(
  475      messages: &[Message],
  476      system_prompt: Option<&SystemPrompt>,
  477      prepared: &PreparedCompactionEnvelope,
  478      billed_input_tokens: Option<u64>,
  479  ) -> bool {
  480      let config = &prepared.config;
  481      if !config.enabled {
  482          return false;
  483      }
  484      if !compaction_pressure_reached_with_billed(
  485          messages,
  486          system_prompt,
  487          config,
  488          billed_input_tokens,
  489      ) {
  490          return false;
  491      }
  492
  493      // The execution path mechanically prunes old verbose tool results before
  494      // asking the model for a summary. Local pruning alone may be enough to
  495      // clear pressure even when the transcript is too small for an LLM pass.
  496      let mut projected_messages = messages.to_vec();
  497      let pruned_bytes =
  498          prune_tool_results_until(&mut projected_messages, KEEP_RECENT_MESSAGES, |_, _| false);
  499      if pruned_bytes > 0 && !compaction_pressure_reached(&projected_messages, system_prompt, config)
  500      {
  501          return true;
  502      }
  503
  504      if messages.len() < MIN_SUMMARIZE_MESSAGES {
  505          return false;
  506      }
  507
  508      // Reclaimability guard: do not start a pass whose replacement request
  509      // (system prompt + retained user messages + summary allowance)
  510      // cannot get below the trigger, or a large stable prefix would cause
  511      // auto-compaction on every tool step.
  512      estimate_retained_floor_conservative(messages, system_prompt, prepared) < config.token_threshold
  513  }
  514
  515  fn truncate_chars(text: &str, max_chars: usize) -> &str {
  516      if max_chars == 0 {
  517          return "";
  518      }
  519      match text.char_indices().nth(max_chars) {
  520          Some((idx, _)) => &text[..idx],
  521          None => text,
  522      }
  523  }
  524
  525  fn tail_chars(text: &str, max_chars: usize) -> String {
  526      if max_chars == 0 {
  527          return String::new();
  528      }
  529      let total_chars = text.chars().count();
  530      if total_chars <= max_chars {
  531          return text.to_string();
  532      }
  533      let start_char = total_chars.saturating_sub(max_chars);
  534      let start_idx = text
  535          .char_indices()
  536          .nth(start_char)
  537          .map_or(0, |(idx, _)| idx);
  538      text[start_idx..].to_string()
  539  }

  545      args_preview: String,

  549      format!(
  550          "{name}:{}",
  551          serde_json::to_string(input).unwrap_or_else(|_| input.to_string())

  556      let redacted = codewhale_config::persistence::redact_json_secrets(input);
  557      let raw = serde_json::to_string(&redacted).unwrap_or_else(|_| redacted.to_string());
  558      truncate_chars(&raw, 120).to_string()
  559  }

  574                          args_preview: tool_args_preview(input),
  575                      },

  577              }
  578          }
05
L1factmodelContext

05 · 摘要失败时有本地 prune、重试和机械 fallback，并把 live state 重新注入

源码事实

compact_messages_safe 先尝试本地 tool-result prune，再按配置调用摘要，允许 transient retry；摘要 ladder 会检测 degenerate/overflow，必要时回退到机械折叠。CompactionConfig 还携带 mode、permission、background shells、workers 和 open approvals 的 live state，供 successor brief 使用。

白话解释

摘要模型挂了并不会让会话消失；系统会先剪工具输出，摘要不合格就重试，再不行就用规则折叠，并把正在跑的 worker、shell 和审批重新告诉下一任 Agent。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可靠压缩要有非模型 fallback 与运行中世界状态，否则长任务恢复只是“希望摘要没漏掉”。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1172–1225
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1226–1281
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · b63e48331b7d · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/tui/src/compaction.rs:1172–1281固定提交 ↗
 1172  /// Compact messages with retry and backoff for transient errors.
 1173  ///
 1174  /// This function wraps `compact_messages` with retry logic to handle
 1175  /// transient network errors and rate limits. It uses exponential backoff
 1176  /// with delays of 1s, 2s, 4s between retries.
 1177  ///
 1178  /// # Safety
 1179  /// - Never panics
 1180  /// - Never corrupts the original messages (returns error instead)
 1181  /// - Only retries on transient errors (network, rate limit, etc.)
 1182  pub async fn compact_messages_safe(
 1183      client: &dyn ModelClient,
 1184      messages: &[Message],
 1185      config: &CompactionConfig,
 1186      workspace: Option<&Path>,
 1187      external_pins: Option<&[usize]>,
 1188      external_working_set_paths: Option<&[String]>,
 1189  ) -> Result<CompactionResult> {
 1190      const MAX_RETRIES: u32 = 3;
 1191      const BASE_DELAY_MS: u64 = 1000;
 1192
 1193      let was_over_threshold = should_compact(
 1194          messages,
 1195          config,
 1196          workspace,
 1197          external_pins,
 1198          external_working_set_paths,
 1199      );
 1200      let mut pruned_messages = messages.to_vec();
 1201      let mut now_under_threshold = false;
 1202      let mut next_stop_check_bytes = 0usize;
 1203      let pruned_bytes = prune_tool_results_until(
 1204          &mut pruned_messages,
 1205          KEEP_RECENT_MESSAGES,
 1206          |candidate_messages, bytes_saved| {
 1207              if !was_over_threshold || bytes_saved < next_stop_check_bytes {
 1208                  return false;
 1209              }
 1210
 1211              // Stop at the first suffix-side prune check that clears the threshold.
 1212              // The check itself is a full compaction-plan pass, so bound it by saved
 1213              // bytes instead of running it after every candidate in huge sessions.
 1214              next_stop_check_bytes = bytes_saved.saturating_add(TOOL_PRUNE_STOP_CHECK_BYTES);
 1215              now_under_threshold = !should_compact(
 1216                  candidate_messages,
 1217                  config,
 1218                  workspace,
 1219                  external_pins,
 1220                  external_working_set_paths,
 1221              );
 1222              now_under_threshold
 1223          },
 1224      );
 1225      if was_over_threshold && pruned_bytes > 0 && !now_under_threshold {
 1226          // The throttled in-loop check may skip the exact candidate that clears the
 1227          // budget. Do one final pass so a successful local prune still avoids LLM compaction.
 1228          now_under_threshold = !should_compact(
 1229              &pruned_messages,
 1230              config,
 1231              workspace,
 1232              external_pins,
 1233              external_working_set_paths,
 1234          );
 1235      }
 1236
 1237      let compaction_input: &[Message] = if pruned_bytes > 0 {
 1238          logging::info(format!(
 1239              "Local tool-result prune saved {pruned_bytes} bytes before LLM compaction"
 1240          ));
 1241          if was_over_threshold && now_under_threshold {
 1242              return Ok(CompactionResult {
 1243                  messages: sanitize_retained_messages(pruned_messages),
 1244                  summary_prompt: None,
 1245                  retries_used: 0,
 1246              });
 1247          }
 1248          &pruned_messages
 1249      } else {
 1250          messages
 1251      };
 1252
 1253      let mut last_error: Option<anyhow::Error> = None;
 1254
 1255      for attempt in 0..MAX_RETRIES {
 1256          if attempt > 0 {
 1257              // Exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s
 1258              let delay = Duration::from_millis(BASE_DELAY_MS * (1 << (attempt - 1)));
 1259              tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
 1260          }
 1261
 1262          match compact_messages(
 1263              client,
 1264              compaction_input,
 1265              config,
 1266              workspace,
 1267              external_pins,
 1268              external_working_set_paths,
 1269          )
 1270          .await
 1271          {
 1272              Ok((msgs, prompt, removed)) => {
 1273                  drop(removed);
 1274                  return Ok(CompactionResult {
 1275                      messages: sanitize_retained_messages(msgs),
 1276                      summary_prompt: prompt,
 1277                      retries_used: attempt,
 1278                  });
 1279              }
 1280              Err(e) => {
 1281                  // Only retry on transient errors
 1282                  if !is_transient_error(&e) {
 1283                      return Err(e);
 1284                  }
 1285                  last_error = Some(e);
 1286              }
 1287          }
 1288      }
 1289
 1290      Err(last_error
 1291          .unwrap_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("Compaction failed after {MAX_RETRIES} retries")))
 1292  }
 1293
 1294  fn read_workspace_anchors(workspace: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<String> {
 1295      let Some(ws) = workspace else {
 1296          return Vec::new();
 1297      };
 1298
 1299      // Prefer .codewhale, fall back to .deepseek
 1300      let primary = ws.join(".codewhale").join("anchors.md");
 1301      let anchors_path = if primary.exists() {
 1302          primary
 1303      } else {
 1304          ws.join(".deepseek").join("anchors.md")
 1305      };
 1306      let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(anchors_path) else {
 1307          return Vec::new();
 1308      };
 1309
 1310      content
 1311          .split("\n---\n")
 1312          .map(str::trim)
 1313          .filter(|anchor| !anchor.is_empty())
 1314          .map(ToOwned::to_owned)
 1315          .collect()
 1316  }
 1317
 1318  fn anchor_summary_section(workspace: Option<&Path>) -> String {
 1319      let anchors = read_workspace_anchors(workspace);
 1320      if anchors.is_empty() {
 1321          return String::new();
 1322      }
 1323
 1324      let mut section = String::from(
 1325          "## Pinned Facts (User Anchors)\n\n\
 1326           The following facts were explicitly anchored by the user with `/anchor`. \
 1327           Preserve them across compaction cycles.\n\n",
 1328      );
 1329
 1330      for anchor in anchors {
 1331          let _ = writeln!(section, "- {anchor}");
 1332      }
 1333
 1334      section.push_str("\n---\n\n");
 1335      section
 1336  }

 1219  fn is_context_window_error(e: &anyhow::Error) -> bool {

 1235  /// Cache-hit percentage for a compaction summary call.
 1236  ///
 1237  /// Denominator is `input_tokens` (the total prompt size), not
 1238  /// `cache_hit + cache_miss`. Some providers populate
 1239  /// `prompt_cache_hit_tokens` but not `prompt_cache_miss_tokens` — using
 1240  /// the sum as the denominator there reports an inflated 100% even when
 1241  /// most of the prompt was uncached. Anchoring on `input_tokens` matches
 1242  /// how the rest of the codebase (cost reporting, `/cache`) infers
 1243  /// missing miss counts. (#584)
 1244  fn user_text_of(msg: &Message) -> Option<String> {
 1245      if msg.role != "user" {
 1246          return None;
 1247      }
 1248      let text = msg
 1249          .content
 1250          .iter()
 1251          .filter_map(|block| match block {
 1252              ContentBlock::Text { text, .. } => Some(text.as_str()),
 1253              _ => None,
 1254          })
 1255          .collect::<Vec<_>>()
 1256          .join("\n");
 1257      let text = text.trim();
 1258      (!text.is_empty()).then(|| text.to_string())
 1259  }

06
L1factmodelContext

06 · 工具目录用排序、memoization 和有界 LRU 支持 cache-stable prefix

源码事实

ToolRegistry 对 API tools 按名称排序、清理并规范化 schema，注册/删除时清空 cache；PrefixStabilityManager 的 ToolCatalogCache 以工具内容身份为 key，容量固定为 8，只保存 digest，插件 hot reload 或 MCP attach 可以显式 invalidate。

白话解释

HashMap 每次启动的随机顺序不会再把整个 tool schema 变成新前缀；同一工具集合重复检查时只算一次，扩展变化时又能主动失效。

对自研 Harness 的含义

要追求 provider cache 命中，catalog 的排序、schema canonicalization、cache invalidation 和扩展生命周期必须绑定设计。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 200–221
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 222–244
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/tui/src/tools/registry.rs:200–244固定提交 ↗
  200      ///
  201      /// Output is sorted by tool name for **prefix-cache stability** (#263).
  202      /// Rust's `HashMap` uses a randomly-seeded hasher per process, so a raw
  203      /// `self.tools.values()` iteration emits tools in a different order on
  204      /// every `deepseek` launch, invalidating DeepSeek's KV prefix cache for
  205      /// every cross-session resume. Sorting here matches the way Claude Code
  206      /// stabilises its tool array (`assembleToolPool` in their reference).
  207      ///
  208      /// The serialised catalog is memoised on first call and pinned across
  209      /// reads so each tool's `description()` and `input_schema()` are sampled
  210      /// exactly once per registration. MCP adapters whose upstream description
  211      /// drifts on reconnect would otherwise rewrite the catalog mid-session
  212      /// and bust the prefix cache. The cache is invalidated on `register`,
  213      /// `remove`, and `clear`.
  214      #[must_use]
  215      pub fn to_api_tools(&self) -> Vec<Tool> {
  216          self.api_cache
  217              .get_or_init(|| self.build_api_tools())
  218              .clone()
  219      }
  220
  221      fn build_api_tools(&self) -> Vec<Tool> {
  222          let mut tools: Vec<&Arc<dyn ToolSpec>> = self.tools.values().collect();
  223          tools.sort_by(|a, b| a.name().cmp(b.name()));
  224          tools
  225              .into_iter()
  226              .filter(|tool| tool.model_visible())
  227              .map(|tool| {
  228                  let mut schema = tool.input_schema();
  229                  schema_sanitize::sanitize(&mut schema);
  230                  schema_canonicalize::canonicalize_schema(&mut schema);
  231                  Tool {
  232                      tool_type: None,
  233                      name: tool.name().to_string(),
  234                      description: tool.description().to_string(),
  235                      input_schema: schema,
  236                      allowed_callers: Some(vec!["direct".to_string()]),
  237                      defer_loading: Some(tool.defer_loading()),
  238                      input_examples: None,
  239                      strict: None,
  240                      cache_control: None,
  241                  }
  242              })
  243              .collect()
  244      }
  245
  246      fn invalidate_api_cache(&mut self) {
  247          self.api_cache = OnceLock::new();
  248      }
  249
  250      /// Convert tools to API Tool format with optional cache control on the last tool.
  251      #[must_use]
  252      pub fn to_api_tools_with_cache(&self, enable_cache: bool) -> Vec<Tool> {
  253          let mut tools = self.to_api_tools();
  254          if enable_cache && let Some(last) = tools.last_mut() {
  255              last.cache_control = Some(crate::models::CacheControl {
  256                  cache_type: "ephemeral".to_string(),
  257              });
  258          }
  259          tools
  260      }

  262      /// Flatten every registered tool into the exact facts the read-only
  263      /// request projection is allowed to report: name, description, model
  264      /// visibility, declared capabilities, declared approval requirement, and
  265      /// whether the tool came from the plugin surface.
  266      ///
  267      /// This hands out *data*, never tool objects, so the projection layer
  268      /// cannot execute anything. Output is sorted by name and does not touch the
  269      /// registry's own ordering or the memoised API catalog.
  270      #[must_use]
  271      pub fn registry_facts(
  272          &self,
  273          plugin_names: &std::collections::HashSet<String>,
  274      ) -> Vec<crate::tool_inspection::RegistryFacts> {
  275          let mut facts: Vec<crate::tool_inspection::RegistryFacts> = self
  276              .tools
  277              .values()
  278              .map(|tool| crate::tool_inspection::RegistryFacts {
  279                  name: tool.name().to_string(),
  280                  description: tool.description().to_string(),
  281                  model_visible: tool.model_visible(),
  282                  capabilities: tool
  283                      .capabilities()
  284                      .iter()
  285                      .map(|capability| format!("{capability:?}"))
  286                      .collect(),
  287                  approval: format!("{:?}", tool.approval_requirement()),
  288                  plugin: plugin_names.contains(tool.name()),
  289              })
  290              .collect();
  291          facts.sort_by(|a, b| a.name.cmp(&b.name));
  292          facts
  293      }

  295      /// Resolve a non-canonical tool name to a registered canonical name.
  296      ///
  297      /// Runs a deterministic ladder against the registered tool names:
  298      /// 1. Lowercase exact match.
  299      /// 2. Hyphens/spaces → underscores (read-file → read_file).
  300      /// 3. CamelCase → snake_case (ReadFile → read_file).
  301      /// 4. Strip trailing `_tool` / `-tool` suffix (twice).
  302      /// 5. Fuzzy match via simple prefix/suffix similarity.
  303      ///
  304      /// Returns `None` when no resolution is found (let the caller surface
  305      /// "Unknown tool").
  306      #[must_use]
  307      pub fn resolve(&self, requested: &str) -> Option<&str> {
  308          let names: Vec<&str> = self.tools.keys().map(String::as_str).collect();
  309          let lower = requested.to_lowercase();
  310
  311          // 1. ASCII case-insensitive exact
  312          if let Some(n) = names.iter().find(|n| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(requested)) {
  313              return Some(n);
  314          }
  315          // 2. hyphen/space → underscore

  236      /// Convert all tools to API Tool format for sending to the model.
  237      ///
  238      /// Output is sorted by tool name for **prefix-cache stability** (#263).
  239      /// Rust's `HashMap` uses a randomly-seeded hasher per process, so a raw
  240      /// `self.tools.values()` iteration emits tools in a different order on
  241      /// every `deepseek` launch, invalidating DeepSeek's KV prefix cache for
  242      /// every cross-session resume. Sorting here matches the way Claude Code
  243      /// stabilises its tool array (`assembleToolPool` in their reference).
  244      ///
  245      /// The serialised catalog is memoised on first call and pinned across
  246      /// reads so each tool's `description()` and `input_schema()` are sampled
  247      /// exactly once per registration. MCP adapters whose upstream description
  248      /// drifts on reconnect would otherwise rewrite the catalog mid-session
  249      /// and bust the prefix cache. The cache is invalidated on `register`,
  250      /// `remove`, and `clear`.
  251      #[must_use]
  252      pub fn to_api_tools(&self) -> Vec<Tool> {
  253          self.api_cache
  254              .get_or_init(|| self.build_api_tools())
  255              .clone()
  256      }
  257
  258      fn build_api_tools(&self) -> Vec<Tool> {
  259          let read_only_authority = self.context.tool_authority.as_deref().filter(|authority| {
  260              authority.authority == super::spec::ToolMutationAuthority::ReadOnly
  261          });
  262          let evidence_only = read_only_authority.is_some();
  263          let evidence_network = self
  264              .context
  265              .tool_authority
  266              .as_ref()
  267              .is_none_or(|authority| authority.network_access == Some(true));
  268          let mut tools: Vec<&Arc<dyn ToolSpec>> = self.tools.values().collect();
  269          tools.sort_by(|a, b| a.name().cmp(b.name()));
  270          tools
  271              .into_iter()
  272              .filter(|tool| tool.model_visible())
  273              .filter(|tool| {
  274                  read_only_authority.is_none_or(|authority| {
  275                      readonly_evidence_tool(tool.as_ref())
  276                          || (tool.name() == "Run"
  277                              && authority.verification
  278                                  == super::spec::ToolVerificationAuthority::Bounded)
  279                  })
  280              })
  281              .filter(|tool| evidence_network || !matches!(tool.name(), "Web" | "web.run"))
  282              .map(|tool| {
  283                  let mut schema = tool.input_schema();
  284                  if evidence_only {
  285                      project_readonly_evidence_schema(tool.name(), &mut schema);
  286                  }
  287                  schema_sanitize::sanitize(&mut schema);
  288                  schema_canonicalize::canonicalize_schema(&mut schema);
  289                  Tool {
  290                      tool_type: None,
  291                      name: tool.name().to_string(),
  292                      description: tool.description().to_string(),
  293                      input_schema: schema,
  294                      allowed_callers: Some(vec!["direct".to_string()]),
  295                      defer_loading: Some(tool.defer_loading()),
  296                      input_examples: None,
  297                      strict: None,
  298                      cache_control: None,
  299                  }
  300              })
  301              .collect()
  302      }
  303
  304      fn invalidate_api_cache(&mut self) {
  305          self.api_cache = OnceLock::new();
  306      }
  307
  308      /// Convert tools to API Tool format with optional cache control on the last tool.
  309      #[must_use]
  310      pub fn to_api_tools_with_cache(&self, enable_cache: bool) -> Vec<Tool> {
  311          let mut tools = self.to_api_tools();
  312          if enable_cache && let Some(last) = tools.last_mut() {
  313              last.cache_control = Some(crate::models::CacheControl {
  314                  cache_type: "ephemeral".to_string(),
  315              });
07
L1factmodelContext

07 · Goal loop 是持久目标层，不是把 max_steps 放大

源码事实

goal_loop 定义 Active/Completed/Blocked、token/time budget、durable progress 和最多 10 次自动 continuation；决策优先尊重模型终态，再检查预算，再检查 circuit breaker。state store 以 SQL 原子累加 token/time/continuation。

白话解释

一个用户目标可以跨多个 turn 继续，但达到完成、阻塞、花费上限或十次没有终态时都会停下来。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长任务应有独立 goal 状态与跨 turn 预算，不能只靠一次请求的 steps 上限。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–11
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 12–23
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/tui/src/goal_loop.rs:1–23固定提交 ↗
    1  //! Goal loop orchestrator — the persistent-objective control layer (#3215, and
    2  //! its lineage #891 / #1976 / #2058 / #2029).
    3  //!
    4  //! This is the **Workflow goal layer**: the decision core that turns a one-shot
    5  //! `/goal` into a persistent work loop. Given the durable goal status, the
    6  //! accumulated usage (from the per-goal accounting wired in `crates/state`
    7  //! `record_thread_goal_usage`), and a budget, it decides whether to **continue**
    8  //! (re-dispatch another worker turn toward the objective) or **stop** with a
    9  //! terminal status. It is the orchestrator in the Workflow≈ultracode mapping —
   10  //! the loop that fans work out to workers (`worker_profile`) and verifies before
   11  //! committing.
   12  //!
   13  //! Scope: **decision logic + types**. The engine (`core/engine.rs`) reads the
   14  //! `SharedGoalState` snapshot after each turn and calls `decide_continuation`
   15  //! to decide whether to re-dispatch. A small cross-turn circuit breaker keeps
   16  //! an unbounded goal from silently spending forever when the model never emits
   17  //! a terminal signal; explicit token/time budgets still take precedence.
   18
   19  /// Maximum automatic cross-turn continuation passes for one goal.
   20  ///
   21  /// This matches the conservative run-cap used by the peer goal lifecycle while
   22  /// avoiding its much larger classifier/strategist subsystem.
   23  pub const MAX_GOAL_CONTINUATIONS: u32 = 10;
   24
   25  /// Terminal or active state of a persistent goal.
   26  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   27  pub enum GoalRunStatus {
   28      /// Still working toward the objective.
   29      Active,
   30      /// The objective was achieved (the model self-reported done and, ideally, a
   31      /// verifier confirmed — see `GoalGate`).
   32      Completed,
   33      /// The model reported it is blocked and needs the user.
   34      #[allow(dead_code)]
   35      Blocked,
   36  }
   37
   38  /// Why the loop stopped, for a terminal decision.
   39  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   40  pub enum StopReason {
   41      /// Objective achieved.
   42      Completed,
   43      /// Model reported blocked.
   44      #[allow(dead_code)]
   45      Blocked,
   46      /// Token budget exhausted.
   47      TokenBudget,
   48      /// Wall-clock budget exhausted.
   49      TimeBudget,
   50      /// Continuation circuit-breaker tripped (too many continuations without a
   51      /// terminal signal).
   52      ContinuationLimit,
   53  }
   54
   55  /// Accumulated, durable progress for a goal run. Mirrors the fields wired by
   56  /// `crates/state` `record_thread_goal_usage` (tokens_used / time_used_seconds)
   57  /// plus a continuation counter the loop maintains.
   58  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
   59  pub struct GoalProgress {
   60      pub tokens_used: u64,
   61      pub time_used_seconds: u64,
   62      pub continuations: u32,
   63  }
   64
   65  /// The optional token/time bounds on a goal run. `None` fields mean unbounded
   66  /// for that resource; the fixed continuation circuit breaker still applies.
   67  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   68  pub struct GoalBudget {
   69      pub token_budget: Option<u64>,
   70      pub time_budget_seconds: Option<u64>,
   71  }
   72
   73  impl GoalBudget {
   74      /// No token or time cap. Terminal status, user control, and the fixed
   75      /// continuation circuit breaker still stop the run.
   76      #[allow(dead_code)]
   77      pub const fn unbounded() -> Self {
   78          Self {
   79              token_budget: None,
   80              time_budget_seconds: None,
   81          }
   82      }
   83
   84      /// A token budget only — the loop runs until the model is done or the
   85      /// token budget is exhausted.
   86      #[allow(dead_code)]
   87      pub const fn with_token_budget(token_budget: u64) -> Self {
   88          Self {
   89              token_budget: Some(token_budget),
   90              time_budget_seconds: None,
   91          }
   92      }
   93  }

    1  //! Goal loop orchestrator — the persistent-objective control layer (#3215, and
    2  //! its lineage #891 / #1976 / #2058 / #2029).
    3  //!

    5  //! `/goal` into a persistent work loop. Given the durable goal status, the
    6  //! accumulated usage (from the per-goal accounting wired in `crates/state`
    7  //! `record_thread_goal_usage`), and a budget, it decides whether to **continue**
    8  //! (re-dispatch another worker turn toward the objective) or **stop** with a
    9  //! terminal status. It is the orchestrator in the Workflow≈ultracode mapping —
   10  //! the loop that fans work out to workers (`worker_profile`) and verifies before
   11  //! committing.
   12  //!
   13  //! Scope: **decision logic + types**. The engine (`core/engine.rs`) reads the
   14  //! `SharedGoalState` snapshot after each turn and calls `decide_continuation`
   15  //! to decide whether to re-dispatch. For operate-mode goals the only terminal
   16  //! stops are a verified completion, a blocked report, or the continuation
   17  //! backstop (`[goal] max_continuations`); token/time accounting stays visible
   18  //! as telemetry but does not gate continuation — the run is unbounded like
   19  //! grokbuild (`DEFAULT_AGENT_BUDGET` as call cap) and kimicode swarm
   20  //! (`turnBudget` per-task, resumable after budget-reached). Log when the
   21  //! backstop fires.
   22
   23  /// Default automatic cross-turn continuation policy for one goal run (#5052).
   24  ///
   25  /// Goals are unlimited by default: completion, blocked status, or explicit
   26  /// user control ends the run. Operators who want a circuit breaker can opt in
   27  /// with `[goal] max_continuations`; `0` keeps the default unlimited behavior.
   28  pub const DEFAULT_MAX_GOAL_CONTINUATIONS: u32 = 0;

DIMENSION · EXECUTION

执行环境与沙箱

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factexecution

01 · 默认 sandbox policy 是 workspace-write，但所有政策仍允许全盘读

源码事实

SandboxPolicy 提供 DangerFullAccess、ReadOnly、ExternalSandbox 和 WorkspaceWrite；默认 WorkspaceWrite 只把 cwd/额外 roots（以及可选 /tmp/TMPDIR）设为可写且关闭网络，但 has_full_disk_read_access 对当前所有政策都返回 true。

白话解释

默认不是“只能看到项目”，而是“能看全盘但只能写项目和指定目录”；这对分析工具方便，对密钥读取风险更敏感。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自研要把读取边界、写入边界和网络边界分别建模，不能把 workspace-write 误称为全隔离。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 17–51
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 52–87
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · b63e48331b7d · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/tui/src/sandbox/policy.rs:17–87固定提交 ↗
   17  /// Determines execution restrictions for shell commands.
   18  ///
   19  /// The sandbox policy controls filesystem access, network access, and other
   20  /// system resources for executed commands. Choose the most restrictive policy
   21  /// that still allows your command to function.
   22  #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   23  #[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "kebab-case")]
   24  pub enum SandboxPolicy {
   25      /// No restrictions whatsoever. Use with extreme caution.
   26      ///
   27      /// This policy disables all sandboxing and allows full system access.
   28      /// Only use this when absolutely necessary and the command source is trusted.
   29      #[serde(rename = "danger-full-access")]
   30      DangerFullAccess,
   31
   32      /// Read-only access to the entire filesystem.
   33      ///
   34      /// The process can read any file but cannot write anywhere.
   35      /// Useful for analysis tools that need broad read access.
   36      #[serde(rename = "read-only")]
   37      ReadOnly,
   38
   39      /// Indicates the process is already running in an external sandbox.
   40      ///
   41      /// Use this when CodeWhale is itself running inside a container,
   42      /// VM, or other sandboxed environment. This avoids double-sandboxing
   43      /// which can cause issues.
   44      #[serde(rename = "external-sandbox")]
   45      ExternalSandbox {
   46          /// Whether network access is allowed in the external sandbox.
   47          #[serde(default)]
   48          network_access: bool,
   49      },
   50
   51      /// Read-only filesystem access plus write access to specified directories.
   52      ///
   53      /// This is the default and recommended policy. It allows:
   54      /// - Read access to the entire filesystem (for tools, libraries, etc.)
   55      /// - Write access only to the current working directory and specified roots
   56      /// - Optional network access
   57      #[serde(rename = "workspace-write")]
   58      WorkspaceWrite {
   59          /// Additional directories where writes are allowed.
   60          #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
   61          writable_roots: Vec<PathBuf>,
   62
   63          /// Whether outbound network connections are permitted.
   64          #[serde(default)]
   65          network_access: bool,
   66
   67          /// Exclude TMPDIR from writable paths.
   68          #[serde(default)]
   69          exclude_tmpdir: bool,
   70
   71          /// Exclude /tmp from writable paths.
   72          #[serde(default)]
   73          exclude_slash_tmp: bool,
   74      },
   75  }
   76
   77  impl Default for SandboxPolicy {
   78      /// Returns the default policy: workspace-write with no extra roots and no network.
   79      fn default() -> Self {
   80          SandboxPolicy::WorkspaceWrite {
   81              writable_roots: vec![],
   82              network_access: false,
   83              exclude_tmpdir: false,
   84              exclude_slash_tmp: false,
   85          }
   86      }
   87  }
   88
   89  impl SandboxPolicy {
   90      /// Create a workspace-write policy with network access enabled.
   91      pub fn workspace_with_network() -> Self {
   92          SandboxPolicy::WorkspaceWrite {
   93              writable_roots: vec![],
   94              network_access: true,
   95              exclude_tmpdir: false,
   96              exclude_slash_tmp: false,
   97          }
   98      }
   99
  100      /// Create a workspace-write policy with additional writable directories.
  101      pub fn workspace_with_roots(roots: Vec<PathBuf>, network: bool) -> Self {
  102          SandboxPolicy::WorkspaceWrite {
  103              writable_roots: roots,
  104              network_access: network,
  105              exclude_tmpdir: false,
  106              exclude_slash_tmp: false,
  107          }
  108      }
  109
  110      /// Returns true if the policy allows reading any file on the filesystem.
  111      pub fn has_full_disk_read_access() -> bool {
  112          // All current policies allow full disk read access
  113          true
  114      }
  115
  116      /// Returns true if the policy allows writing to any file on the filesystem.
  117      pub fn has_full_disk_write_access(&self) -> bool {
  118          matches!(
  119              self,
  120              SandboxPolicy::DangerFullAccess | SandboxPolicy::ExternalSandbox { .. }
  121          )
  122      }
  123
  124      /// Returns true if the policy allows outbound network connections.
  125      pub fn has_network_access(&self) -> bool {
  126          match self {
  127              SandboxPolicy::DangerFullAccess => true,
  128              SandboxPolicy::ReadOnly => false,
  129              SandboxPolicy::ExternalSandbox { network_access }
  130              | SandboxPolicy::WorkspaceWrite { network_access, .. } => *network_access,
  131          }
  132      }
  133
  134      /// Returns true if the sandbox should be applied (not bypassed).
  135      pub fn should_sandbox(&self) -> bool {
  136          !matches!(
  137              self,
  138              SandboxPolicy::DangerFullAccess | SandboxPolicy::ExternalSandbox { .. }
  139          )
  140      }
  141
  142      /// Get the list of writable roots for this policy.
  143      ///
  144      /// This includes:
  145      /// - The current working directory
  146      /// - Any explicitly specified `writable_roots`
  147      /// - /tmp (unless excluded)
  148      /// - TMPDIR (unless excluded)
  149      ///
  150      /// For policies with full write access, returns an empty vec since
  151      /// there's no need to enumerate specific paths.
  152      pub fn get_writable_roots(&self, cwd: &Path) -> Vec<WritableRoot> {
  153          match self {
  154              // Full write access or read-only - no enumeration needed
  155              SandboxPolicy::DangerFullAccess
  156              | SandboxPolicy::ExternalSandbox { .. }
  157              | SandboxPolicy::ReadOnly => vec![],
  158
