  281          "child wall-time budget exhausted (limit: {}s); raise it with wall_time_secs or split the work into smaller independent tasks",
  282          limit.as_secs()
  283      )
  284  }

  286  // arguments, especially write_file content. The API bills generated tokens, not
  287  // the requested ceiling.
  288  const SUBAGENT_TRANSIENT_PROVIDER_MAX_RETRIES: u32 = 2;
  289  const SUBAGENT_TRANSIENT_PROVIDER_INITIAL_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_millis(250);
  290  /// Per-step API-call timeout retry budget. A `create_message` call that
  291  /// exceeds `step_api_timeout` is re-issued up to this many times (with
  292  /// exponential backoff) before the step is interrupted with a preserved
  293  /// checkpoint. Dogfooding showed a single 120s stall wiping out every child
  294  /// in a fan-out one by one even though the provider call was live-but-slow,
  295  /// so a timeout now gets the same retry dignity as a transient provider
  296  /// error (kimi-code comparison, FINISH-0.9.4 entry #40).
  297  const SUBAGENT_API_TIMEOUT_MAX_RETRIES: u32 = 5;
  298  /// Initial backoff for a timed-out per-step API call; doubles per retry and
  299  /// is capped at [`SUBAGENT_API_TIMEOUT_MAX_BACKOFF`].

L1factcollaboration

04 · 子 Agent 可在父 workspace 内运行，也可创建隔离 Git worktree

源码事实

prepare_child_workspace 会 canonicalize requested cwd 并拒绝离开 parent workspace；传入 worktree 请求时验证 repo/branch，执行 git worktree add -b，默认根目录为 .codewhale-worktrees。

白话解释

不指定 worktree 时，子 Agent 只能在父项目里选一个存在的目录；需要并行改代码时，可以让系统创建独立分支和工作树。

对自研 Harness 的含义

协作执行应把 cwd containment 和 worktree isolation 做成显式参数，避免“每个 child 都能随便 cd”。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–23
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 24–47
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   16  pub(super) struct SubAgentWorktreeRequest {
   17      pub(super) branch: Option<String>,
   18      pub(super) path: Option<PathBuf>,
   19      pub(super) base_ref: Option<String>,
   20  }
   21
   22  pub(super) fn prepare_child_workspace(
   23      parent_workspace: &Path,
   24      requested_cwd: Option<&Path>,
   25      worktree: Option<&SubAgentWorktreeRequest>,
   26      session_name: Option<&str>,
   27      agent_type: &FleetRole,
   28  ) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>, ToolError> {
   29      let discovery_anchor = if let Some(requested_cwd) = requested_cwd {
   30          validate_existing_child_cwd(parent_workspace, requested_cwd)?
   31      } else {
   32          parent_workspace
   33              .canonicalize()
   34              .unwrap_or_else(|_| parent_workspace.to_path_buf())
   35      };
   36
   37      if let Some(worktree) = worktree {
   38          return create_isolated_worktree(&discovery_anchor, worktree, session_name, agent_type)
   39              .map(Some);
   40      }
   41
   42      if requested_cwd.is_some() {
   43          return Ok(Some(discovery_anchor));
   44      }
   45
   46      Ok(None)
   47  }
   48
   49  fn validate_existing_child_cwd(
   50      parent_workspace: &Path,
   51      requested_cwd: &Path,
   52  ) -> Result<PathBuf, ToolError> {
   53      let resolved = if requested_cwd.is_absolute() {
   54          requested_cwd.to_path_buf()
   55      } else {
   56          parent_workspace.join(requested_cwd)
   57      };
   58      let canonical = resolved.canonicalize().map_err(|e| {
   59          ToolError::invalid_input(format!(
   60              "Invalid cwd '{}': {e} (path may not exist yet — use worktree=true to let Codewhale create an isolated checkout)",
   61              requested_cwd.display()
   62          ))
   63      })?;
   64      let workspace_canonical = parent_workspace
   65          .canonicalize()
   66          .unwrap_or_else(|_| parent_workspace.to_path_buf());
   67      if !canonical.starts_with(&workspace_canonical) {
   68          return Err(ToolError::invalid_input(format!(
   69              "cwd must be inside the parent workspace: {} is not under {}",
   70              canonical.display(),
   71              workspace_canonical.display()
   72          )));
   73      }
   74      Ok(canonical)
   75  }

   83      let repo_root = git_repo_root(parent_workspace)?;

   86          .clone()
   87          .unwrap_or_else(|| default_worktree_branch(session_name, agent_type));
   88      validate_git_branch_name(&repo_root, &branch)?;

   98      if let Some(parent) = worktree_path.parent() {
   99          fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|err| {
  100              ToolError::execution_failed(format!(
  101                  "Failed to create worktree parent '{}': {err}",
  102                  parent.display()
  103              ))
  104          })?;

  113          path_arg,
  114          base_ref,
  115      ];
  116      run_git_checked(&repo_root, &args, "create sub-agent worktree")?;
  117      worktree_path.canonicalize().map_err(|err| {
  118          ToolError::execution_failed(format!(

05 · Fleet 把 worker host、运行状态、artifact 和控制面做成可观测协议

源码事实

FleetHostAdapter 抽象 local process 与 SSH host，统一 start/read_status/read_logs/interrupt/restart/stop/cleanup，worker status 带 pid/exit/memory/retryable；fleet control 使用 workspace 下的 .codewhale/fleet.jsonl，CLI 与 TUI 复用同一 durable ledger/renderers，worker/artifact 行数有上限。

白话解释

Fleet 不要求控制台知道 worker 是本机进程还是 SSH；它只消费统一的状态和 artifact 事件，状态页不会因为刷新而偷偷创建空 ledger。

对自研 Harness 的含义

远程协作面应把 host adapter 与产品控制面分开，并让状态查询默认为纯观察。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–2
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 3–5
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

DIMENSION · INSTRUCTIONS

全部指令与扩展面

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factinstructions

01 · Skills 同时兼容生态目录与 CodeWhale owned roots，支持 explicit-only 和 locale 描述

源码事实

SkillDiscoveryMode 在 Compatible 与 CodeWhaleOnly 之间切换；Skill 结构记录 description/localized_descriptions、ModelAndUser/ExplicitOnly invocation、aliases、实际 SKILL.md path 与 Native/Plugin source。目录遍历有深度、隐藏目录、canonical visited 和 shadowing warning。

白话解释

它能读取 `.agents`、Claude、OpenCode、Cursor 等兼容 Skills，也能只读自己的 `.codewhale/skills`；技能可以不出现在模型菜单里，只有用户点名才加载。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Skills 是 prompt 注入面，应有来源、调用方式、别名和路径 provenance，而不是简单把所有 Markdown 拼进 system prompt。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 131–176
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 177–224
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  171  /// Parsed representation of a SKILL.md definition.
  172  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  173  pub struct Skill {
  174      pub name: String,
  175      /// Default (language-neutral, usually English) description.
  176      pub description: String,
  177      /// Optional locale-specific descriptions, keyed by lowercased locale tag
  178      /// (e.g. `zh`, `zh-hant`, `ja`). Populated from `description_<tag>:`
  179      /// frontmatter keys so a skill author can ship a shorter, native-language
  180      /// description for non-English sessions (saves prompt tokens; see #3354).
  181      pub localized_descriptions: HashMap<String, String>,
  182      /// Whether the skill may be selected from the model's catalogue or only
  183      /// loaded after an explicit user request. Missing metadata preserves the
  184      /// historical model-and-user behavior.
  185      pub invocation: SkillInvocation,
  186      /// Alternate names accepted by `load_skill`; aliases never become extra
  187      /// prompt entries, so they do not inflate the catalogue or create a
  188      /// second instruction surface.
  189      pub aliases: Vec<String>,
  190      pub body: String,
  191      /// On-disk path to the `SKILL.md` this was loaded from. The directory
  192      /// name can differ from the frontmatter `name` for community installs
  193      /// or manually-placed skills, so callers must use this rather than
  194      /// reconstructing `<dir>/<name>/SKILL.md`.
  195      pub path: PathBuf,
  196      pub source: SkillSource,
  197  }
  198
  199  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  200  pub enum SkillInvocation {
  201      ModelAndUser,
  202      ExplicitOnly,
  203  }
  204
  205  impl SkillInvocation {
  206      fn from_frontmatter(value: Option<&str>) -> Self {
  207          match value.map(str::trim).map(|value| value.to_ascii_lowercase()) {
  208              Some(value) if value == "explicit-only" || value == "explicit_only" => {
  209                  Self::ExplicitOnly
  210              }
  211              _ => Self::ModelAndUser,
  212          }
  213      }
  214  }

  216  #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  217  pub enum SkillSource {
  218      Native,
  219      Plugin {
  220          plugin_id: String,
  221          plugin_name: String,
  222          authority: Box<crate::plugins::types::PluginAuthority>,
  223      },
  224  }
  225
  226  impl Skill {
  227      /// Pick the best description for a session `locale_tag`, falling back to the
  228      /// default `description` when no localized variant matches.
  229      ///
  230      /// Order: exact (lowercased) tag match, then the primary language subtag
  231      /// (so `en-us` → `en`, `pt-br` → `pt`, `zh-cn` → `zh`), then default.
  232      ///
  233      /// Chinese is the one place where the primary-subtag fallback would be
  234      /// *wrong*: Traditional and Simplified are written differently, so a
  235      /// Traditional tag (`zh-hant`, or the Traditional regions `zh-tw` / `zh-hk`
  236      /// / `zh-mo`) must NOT borrow a Simplified `description_zh`. Those match only
  237      /// an exact `description_zh-hant`-style key, else the default. Simplified
  238      /// tags (`zh`, `zh-hans`, `zh-cn`, …) still fold to `description_zh`.
  239      #[must_use]
  240      pub fn description_for_locale(&self, locale_tag: &str) -> &str {
  241          if self.localized_descriptions.is_empty() {
  242              return &self.description;
  243          }
  244          let normalized = locale_tag.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
  245          if let Some(desc) = self.localized_descriptions.get(&normalized) {
  246              return desc;
  247          }
  248          if let Some((primary, _)) = normalized.split_once('-') {
  249              // Don't let a Traditional-Chinese session fall back to a Simplified
  250              // (`zh`) description — different written form, not just a region.
  251              let traditional_chinese = primary == "zh"
  252                  && (normalized.contains("hant")
  253                      || normalized.ends_with("-tw")
  254                      || normalized.ends_with("-hk")
  255                      || normalized.ends_with("-mo"));
  256              if !traditional_chinese && let Some(desc) = self.localized_descriptions.get(primary) {
  257                  return desc;
  258              }
  259          }
  260          &self.description
  261      }
  262  }
  263
  264  /// Collection of discovered skills.
  265  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
  266  pub struct SkillRegistry {
  267      skills: Vec<Skill>,
  268      warnings: Vec<String>,
  269  }
  270
  271  /// Cheap metadata stamp used to validate one watched discovery path.
  272  ///
  273  /// Some filesystems expose modification times at a coarse resolution. Keeping
  274  /// the file length alongside the timestamp lets an immediate content rewrite
  275  /// invalidate the cache even when the timestamp is unchanged.
  276  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  277  pub(crate) struct WatchedPathStamp {
  278      modified: Option<std::time::SystemTime>,
  279      len: u64,
  280  }
  281
  282  /// One cached discovery's watched filesystem entries: a path and the metadata
  283  /// stamp observed during the validating walk. `None` means the path was
  284  /// unreadable at walk time; any later readability or metadata change
  285  /// invalidates the entry.
  286  pub(crate) type WatchedPaths = Vec<(PathBuf, Option<WatchedPathStamp>)>;
  287
  288  pub(crate) fn watched_path_stamp(path: &Path) -> Option<WatchedPathStamp> {
  289      fs::metadata(path).ok().map(|metadata| WatchedPathStamp {
  290          modified: metadata.modified().ok(),
  291          len: metadata.len(),
  292      })
  293  }

02
L1factinstructions

02 · 插件 trust 和 enable 分离，内容/能力 hash 变化会自动失信

源码事实

PluginRegistry 在加载 persisted state 时比较 content_hash/capability_hash，变化标为 ContentChanged/CapabilitiesChanged；trust 会 stage bundle、写 receipt 但强制 enabled=false；enable 要求 trusted、staged snapshot、host applicability 和 supported capabilities 全部满足。

白话解释

看过插件不等于立即打开插件；信任是审核记录，启用是另一个明确动作。插件内容或能力变了，旧审核不能继续生效。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自研插件市场要把 review receipt、runtime snapshot、enable state 和 capability drift 做成不同状态，禁止一次“安装”打开全部能力。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 123–145
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 146–169
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/tui/src/plugins/registry.rs:123–169固定提交 ↗
  123      fn register_loaded(&mut self, plugin: LoadedPlugin) {
  124          self.names
  125              .insert(plugin.name().to_string(), plugin.id.clone());
  126          self.plugins.insert(plugin.id.clone(), plugin);
  127      }
  128
  129      fn apply_state(&mut self) {
  130          let state_path = self.state_path.clone();
  131          for (id, plugin) in &mut self.plugins {
  132              let persisted = self.state.plugins.get(id);
  133              plugin.state_generation = persisted.map_or(0, |state| state.generation);
  134              plugin.enabled = persisted.is_some_and(|state| state.enabled);
  135              plugin.trust_status = match persisted.and_then(|state| state.trust.as_ref()) {
  136                  Some(receipt) if receipt.capability_hash != plugin.capability_hash => {
  137                      PluginTrustStatus::CapabilitiesChanged
  138                  }
  139                  Some(receipt) if receipt.content_hash != plugin.content_hash => {
  140                      PluginTrustStatus::ContentChanged
  141                  }
  142                  Some(_) => PluginTrustStatus::Trusted,
  143                  None => PluginTrustStatus::NeverReviewed,
  144              };
  145              if self.state_error.is_some() {
  146                  plugin.enabled = false;
  147                  plugin.trust_status = PluginTrustStatus::NeverReviewed;
  148              }
  149              plugin.staged_root = state_path.as_deref().and_then(|state_path| {
  150                  let staged_root = runtime_stage_path(state_path, id, &plugin.content_hash);
  151                  staged_bundle_matches(&staged_root, &plugin.content_hash, &plugin.capability_hash)
  152                      .then_some(staged_root)
  153              });
  154              if let Some(staged_root) = plugin.staged_root.clone() {
  155                  match super::discovery::load_staged_skill_snapshots(
  156                      &staged_root,
  157                      &plugin.content_hash,
  158                      &plugin.capability_hash,
  159                  ) {
  160                      Ok(snapshots) => plugin.skill_snapshots = snapshots,
  161                      Err(error) => {
  162                          plugin.staged_root = None;
  163                          plugin.enabled = false;
  164                          plugin.diagnostics.push(PluginDiagnostic::error(
  165                              "staged-skill-invalid",
  166                              format!("Plugin runtime Skill snapshot is fail-closed: {error}"),
  167                              Some(staged_root),
  168                          ));
  169                      }
  170                  }
  171              }
  172          }
  173      }
  174
  175      #[must_use]
  176      pub fn workspace(&self) -> &Path {
  177          &self.workspace
  178      }

  180      /// Re-discover for a new workspace using the immutable pre-dotenv roots
  181      /// and environment. Registries without a context are test/ad-hoc values
  182      /// and remain fail-closed instead of consulting ambient process state.
  183      #[must_use]
  184      pub fn rediscover_for_workspace(&self, workspace: &Path) -> std::sync::Arc<Self> {
  185          self.discovery_context.as_ref().map_or_else(
  186              || std::sync::Arc::new(Self::empty(workspace)),

  195              .map(|context| context.host_environment())

  220      #[must_use]
  221      pub fn list(&self) -> Vec<&LoadedPlugin> {
  222          let mut plugins = self.plugins.values().collect::<Vec<_>>();
  223          plugins.sort_by(|left, right| {
  224              left.scope
  225                  .cmp(&right.scope)
  226                  .then_with(|| left.name().cmp(right.name()))
  227                  .then_with(|| left.id.cmp(&right.id))
  228          });
  229          plugins
  230      }
  231
  232      #[must_use]
  233      pub fn get(&self, selector: &str) -> Option<&LoadedPlugin> {
  234          let id = self.resolve_id(selector)?;
  235          self.plugins.get(id)
  236      }

  240          self.list()

03
L1factinstructions

03 · Hook 事件覆盖 turn/tool/subagent，ToolCallBefore 失败可按 strict gate fail-closed

源码事实

HookEvent 契约列出 session_start/end、turn_end、message_submit、tool_call_before/after、mode_change、on_error、subagent_spawn/complete、shell_env 共 11 种事件。ToolCallBefore 在 blocking worker 执行，合并 deny/ask/updatedInput/additionalContext；严格且 continue_on_error=false 的 hook 失去 verdict 会阻断工具。

白话解释

Hook 不只是“执行一个脚本”：它能在工具落地前改参数、要求审批或阻止调用；Hook executor 崩掉时，严格 gate 不会被当成允许。

对自研 Harness 的含义

扩展 hook 要明确同步/后台语义、超时、失联处理和 input re-prepare，否则自定义治理逻辑会变成绕过点。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 656–675
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 676–697
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  656      /// `config.toml`, in `/hooks events`, and in `docs/HOOKS.md`. A rename is
  657      /// a breaking change, and a new variant must be added deliberately.
  658      #[test]
  659      fn all_eleven_event_names_are_stable_and_exhaustive() {
  660          let names: Vec<&str> = ALL_HOOK_EVENTS.iter().map(|e| e.as_str()).collect();
  661          assert_eq!(
  662              names,
  663              vec![
  664                  "session_start",
  665                  "session_end",
  666                  "turn_end",
  667                  "message_submit",
  668                  "tool_call_before",
  669                  "tool_call_after",
  670                  "mode_change",
  671                  "on_error",
  672                  "subagent_spawn",
  673                  "subagent_complete",
  674                  "shell_env",
  675              ]
  676          );
  677
  678          // Exhaustiveness: every variant appears exactly once. The `match` here
  679          // fails to compile if a variant is added without updating the list.
  680          for event in ALL_HOOK_EVENTS {
  681              let covered = match event {
  682                  HookEvent::SessionStart
  683                  | HookEvent::SessionEnd
  684                  | HookEvent::TurnEnd
  685                  | HookEvent::MessageSubmit
  686                  | HookEvent::ToolCallBefore
  687                  | HookEvent::ToolCallAfter
  688                  | HookEvent::ModeChange
  689                  | HookEvent::OnError
  690                  | HookEvent::SubagentSpawn
  691                  | HookEvent::SubagentComplete
  692                  | HookEvent::ShellEnv => true,
  693              };
  694              assert!(covered);
  695          }
  696          let unique: std::collections::HashSet<&str> = names.iter().copied().collect();
  697          assert_eq!(unique.len(), 11);
  698      }

  711              let hook: Hook = toml::from_str(&toml_src).expect("hook parses from minimal toml");
  712              assert_eq!(hook.event, event);

11
DIMENSION · RECOVERY

恢复、测试与取舍

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L3factrecovery

01 · 关键治理模块都有契约测试，但测试覆盖不等于运行时强制全开

源码事实

仓库为 compaction、bwrap、execution_envelope、plugins、MCP、Skills、subagent 和 core engine 提供独立测试模块；测试能证明设计意图和回归约束，不能证明用户一定启用了 OS sandbox、插件或某个 provider。

白话解释

源码里不仅有实现，也有大量“规则不能被改坏”的测试；但测试通过只说明代码在测试场景下遵守规则，不能替代生产环境 capability 检查。

对自研 Harness 的含义

评测体系应把单元/契约测试、真实 sandbox probe、provider replay 和端到端交付演练分开统计。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 2135–2140
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 2141–2148
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • 本账本未执行全量 cargo test；这里只记录仓库中可定位的测试实现。
  • 部分测试包含在超大模块底部，报告代码摘录会按行号折叠。
